## Status vocabulary

- **VERIFIED-HISTORICAL-BYTES**: new exact-ID retrieval matches a historical SHA-256.
- **VERIFIED-CURRENT-OBJECT**: fetched using the exact recorded Drive ID; newly hashed/probed, but no historical checksum available.
- **VERIFIED-SUPPLIED-COPY**: supplied by the user in response to the exact-ID request, with byte size matching independently retrieved Drive metadata. A historical/remote checksum was not available to prove byte identity independently.
- **SUBSTITUTED-TRIMMED**: user-declared derivative with its own hash and measured duration; not original bytes.
- **HISTORICAL-ONLY** applies to original claims of inspection and old measurements where not reproduced; **UNRESOLVED** applies to residual equivalence or inspection questions, never to an invented zero measurement.

A source hash or measurement is not watching/listening. New visual inspection is logged separately; no new direct listening or complete continuous watching is certified. Historical qualitative analysis is preserved and is neither proved nor disproved by missing working outputs.

## Authorized execution source distinction

The retained tables above are the supplied rebuild's acquisition/measurement records, not a claim of fresh processing by the current reviewer. H04's retained measurement input remains derivative SHA-256 `de92afafb3181342407ab9405f4951e820bfdafaad5b1e9538ec9d7f73ea826e` (3316.029383 s). The original is a separate input: SHA-256 `4dc80d8f2431fe8780faf59fa6b9f372f0fafcb599607ce9e04ead81bc4d4468`, 545587232 bytes, 3516.615692 s. Every retained audio/video packet shares payload and clock with the original prefix; no old full-file number is relabeled. Current individual source verification and probe records are in [execution source verification](SUPPORTING_DATA/EXECUTION_SOURCE_VERIFICATION.md).

## Authorized execution H26 source-form qualification

H26 (`1laiDZzcPX6UA-Hz9NHaoAvdc8bX2Q6nW`) preserves every original manifest field and existing numerical group membership. Its 20 newly viewed frames show scenery/lyrics/title imagery, not performer bodies. The historical label `duet_performance` therefore must not be expanded into evidence of duet choreography or bodily distribution. H28 provides the current two-body visual anchor. See [the direct source-form review](SUPPORTING_DATA/EXECUTION_MUSIC_AND_BREADTH_REVIEW.md). Source count stays 33.
